## Method

- Rows are the previously reviewed manual judgments (promote and supersede) whose candidate target was retired by the A16 move; each now binds the literal history snapshot of the whole prior candidate file. For every row I recomputed the source unit digest from the legacy file bytes and the target (`Literal Snapshot`) digest from the snapshot file at HEAD with `extractMarkdownConservationUnits`, located the previous reviewed candidate unit at 7880fbc (digest equals the reviewed binding) and proved it lies byte-for-byte inside the snapshot body, compared source with the previous candidate unit after whitespace normalisation (identical, contained, or differing), and checked disposition, claim kind and the retirement reference. All rows whose source differs from the previous candidate unit (the supersede rows), every row without a previous-state record and the SVG row (106 rows in the whole area) were read in full: the differences were categorised by a script (migration-status blockquote dropped, promotion-metadata fence added, link targets rewritten, labelled additions) and each case was read by a sub-reviewer and by me; the residual differences (dead link turned into labelled plain text; labelled enum value `materialized`) are named in the rationales.
- Row verdicts combine two independent questions. (1) Is the verbatim carriage in the history snapshot correct and the rationale accurate? That passed for every row. (2) Is moving this unit to non-authority history the right disposition? Rows whose target file, or whose cut section of a retained file, the whole-area file review (review-2whole-files.md) found to be still-current material are `rework` (live material labelled as retired history), and rows whose file or section is an owner question are `hold`. Nothing is lost in either case: the snapshots carry the bytes.
- No row was approved on the strength of a prior approval; prior reviewer identities are cited only as the previous reviewed state.

## Result

- Rows: 424; ok 297; rework 92; hold 35.
- By cause: ok/none 297; hold/file 35; rework/file 92.

## Disagreements and unproven assertions

- None on carriage: all 424 source digests, target digests and containment checks pass. The only open items are the classification defects counted above (see review-2whole-files.md for the evidence per file).
